4241 — resistance band shuttle

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4241 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Quick side-to-side steps against band tension. It trains lateral leg power, agility and glute strength.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Anchor a band at hip height or place it around your waist with a partner or post. 4241.json#/instructions/0
  2. Get into a slight squat with your chest up. 4241.json#/instructions/1
  3. Shuffle sideways several steps against the tension. 4241.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your feet from crossing. 4241.json#/instructions/3
  5. Shuffle back the other way under control. 4241.json#/instructions/4
  6. Stay low throughout. 4241.json#/instructions/5

4242 — broad jump to vertical

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4242 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A forward jump followed straight away by a jump up. It trains explosive power in two directions.

Complete ordered source instructions

4247 — landmine romanian deadlift v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4247 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A Romanian deadlift using the landmine bar for an angled load. It builds the hamstrings and glutes with a good hip hinge.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold the end of the landmine bar with both hands in front of your thighs. Stand with soft knees and a flat back.

  1. Hold the end of the landmine bar with both hands in front of your thighs. 4247.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with soft knees and a flat back. 4247.json#/instructions/1
  3. Push your hips back and slide the bar down your legs. 4247.json#/instructions/2

A crunch holding a dumbbell straight above your chest. It loads the abs while your arms stay locked.
